- **CTO-275 — RAKU GATHER/TAKE AND THE SEQUENCE OPERATOR ARE LAZY GENERATOR BOXES, LANDED AS SCRIP dbd4b7cf1 (on 48977712f; 2026-10-10 17:4x CDT, SEPTET; row raku-gather-take-and-the-sequence-operator-are-lazy-generator-boxes-with-no-cap-ceo-1600, CEO-1600 step 2, the escape boundary CEO-1619; closed by computed done).** BUILT WHOLE after the small handoff experiment (a gather as a for source, 4dca4b98c, smoked in both modes): a gather body is a hoisted block that is a GENERATOR PROC on Icon's suspend road (take = IR_SUSPEND; rk_mark_generators makes exactly the gather blocks generators); a for pumps it through IR_PROC_GEN with the loop's last/next wired to the call; array assignment, say, a literal subscript, head(n) and the eager methods pump with a bound; an ESCAPE (scalar, return, argument, take, infinite sequence into an array, a dynamic-take body) is a Seq -- a DT_A block proto Seq {closure, DT_RAW handle, state, cache List} pulled one value at a time through rt_proc_call_gen_h / rt_proc_resume_frame_h on its own coroutine stack under an rk_cbh_t hazard; rk_av reifies whole, the indexers, slicers, head and __rk_iter_has reify what they need, __rk_to_array keeps a Seq lazy; a for over a value pulls per step so a lazy Seq interleaves with its producer; a take in a nested block or a called sub is __rk_take_dyn -> rt_genp_deliver_γ; the parser's __rk_seq is desugared in the lowerer into a gather (seeds, the deduced arithmetic/geometric/string step from pure leaves, the seed block as a TT_INVOKE by arity, the endpoint value/block/Whatever, the trailing elements) and rk_sequence with its caps is deleted; map/grep/first on a lazy receiver are generator stages. FOUND AND CURED ON THE WAY, two shared-node defects no language had reached: (1) the Raku lowerer's build() marks a γ edge into a generator kind as a resume, so a generator call with arguments entered its β first (act cell 0, fault 0x28) -- the argument chain ends at a GOTO trampoline; (2) a sub holding a generator call is a pinned-base graph whose prologue reads parameters from a caller-pushed block and whose release pops it, while the direct-call stub's zframe branch and the generic rt_proc_call_open_detN road staged them -- the stub builds the block for a pinned non-Icon graph and a non-pinned caller of a pinned block callee takes the det-block arm with the block alone (the det-block callee consumes two wire cells from the stack, the pinned one takes them in rcx/rdx). Also cured: next and last inside a for over an array value (the internal TT_FOR_RANGE had no loop context). MEASURED: the row's LEDGER (DONE-WHEN 5/5 both modes, red 5/5 on the base; the new gate 39 arms; the two existing Raku gates pass; two-tree 117/117 Prolog and 167/185 others byte-identical with 7 Snocone parsers differing only in a text-medium tag; 142 touched gates with every non-green identical to the control; preflight 77 arms 1 corpus red, area smoke 1211 entries 0 new red). - **CTO-252 — A2 (1) MEASURED BEFORE BUILT: THE 73 MATCH-CLASS SITES ARE CONTROL-NESTED EXPRESSION BOXES, NOT AN EMISSION SPAN; THE NESTING COMES FROM IR REACHABILITY (2026-10-04 ~01:xx-02:xx CDT, TENET; branch cto-chain-over-the-maps-2026-10-03 at 042804c69 over origin 5e38ea3c2, UNPUSHED).** I built the match-frame rule the obvious way first -- a record before the match prologue's push capturing the enclosing depth, every site built between MATCH_BEGIN and MATCH_END carrying it with its nesting level, the walker climbing the saved frame pointers -- and the check moved none of the 73: a join by absolute pc between the emitter's site report (now with the box kind and the match depth at build) and the check's per-site line names them as call and binop boxes of main built OUTSIDE the match span (match depth 0 at build) and polled INSIDE the live match frame (rbp = main base - 24, the truth 352 bytes under the planner's depth). A match is a control region -- MATCH_BEGIN's gamma enters the pattern (lower_snobol4.c 2229), MATCH_END releases (2269) -- and the expression boxes an eager pattern evaluates belong to it by the gamma and omega edges, not by node order; deferred expressions compile at run time as map-less fragments and are the frameless class instead. The design that follows is in the row's NEXT: reachability from each MATCH_BEGIN over gamma/omega edges to its MATCH_END marks every node with its innermost match and nesting; a site's rule-6 depth is resolved at the end of the graph from its match's recorded enclosing depth, because subject and replacement boxes are built before their match plays; the walker climbs the saved frame pointers n-1 times and applies the outermost enclosing rule, with a consistency check that the climb inside the segment takes exactly that many steps. The reading of CEO-1492 asked of the ceo (a pinned frame's map word read by address, never scanned) decides the thunk-context class, not this one. Sitting closed on this measurement; the branch carries everything.

- **CTO-250 — LANDING 1 STEP A: THE SITE TABLE, THE RSP TRACKER AND THE DIFFERENTIAL CHECK ARE BUILT AND MEASURED OVER ALL 103 WITNESSES; THE THREE CLASSES LEFT ARE NAMED AND COUNTED (2026-10-03 ~20:0x-22:1x CDT, TENET; branch cto-chain-over-the-maps-2026-10-03 over SCRIP 8d8e4b866, UNPUSHED; CEO-1492/1493).** The per-site table of ARCH-GC 13.3 exists in both media: a zero-byte site record after every emitted call's bytes, played into the absolute return address (binary) or a label (text), gathered per graph and emitted after the frame map as {n, map, {pc, kind|rule|depth}}, installed beside every rt_gc_frame_maps_add and dropped beside the maps drop in bb_pool_release, looked up by binary search over PC-sorted per-graph blocks. The base rules are measured: pinned zframe and Icon host (base = rbp), Icon generator (rbp - ft), blob (rbp - frame_bytes), spine (base = R + the planner's run depth under the node + the program-order rsp delta), aligned call ([R] + depth), unknown (refused). THE ONE THING THAT MADE IT RIGHT: the depth is fixed at PLAY time, in program order, by a tracker over zero-byte records (push/pop/sub/add rsp, aligned regions, unconditional jumps, and jump/define snapshots that restore the delta at a label reached only by jump) -- a template string's operator+ operands are evaluated in an unspecified order, so nothing at build time can see the carve before the call, and the planner's op_zdepth is the node's own record, not the run's depth: the differential check (SCRIP_GC_CHAIN_CHECK, every poll hands the collector its R, pc and rbp; the chain's first frame must be one of the marker scan's frames) read the SNOBOL4 truth as 32/48/112/144 at successive polls and the tracker now matches it everywhere. THE SWEEP, 103 gc_witnesses m3 at 64 KB stress 1 (before the Raku fix): 40 clean; sno 1 of 42, icn 29 of 40, pl 8 of 9, raku 0 of 10, pas 1 of 1, sc 0 of 1; five witnesses in m4 agree with m3 exactly. CLASSES LEFT: (a) Raku -- the planner's -1 sentinel for an unplanned node read as a depth, FIXED (rk_list_array_marker 1529 ok 0 mismatch); (b) NO SITE, 8089 polls in 60 witnesses, all inside map-less code entered from asm leaves that keep no frame pointer (SNOBOL4 deferred-expression thunks and EVAL fragments, 1-3 per witness; the Icon scan asm: hb_scan_nested 6021) -- the design's road shapes (13.4): every asm road into emitted code builds a frame or registers its landing with a stated R relation; (c) Icon host-pinned local procedures run in main's frame, their table must carry the host's map; (d) a site after a frame's release belongs to the caller. Not started: link following against the full marker set (A2), then STEP B. Everything is in the row's ledger with the numbers; nothing is pushed.

- **CTO-146 — hq_snobol4's r9 SAFEPOINT-ASYMMETRY FINDING IS REFUTED BY MEASUREMENT, AND THE TWO REDS IT CITED ARE SNOBOL4 DEFECTS OUTSIDE THE COLLECTOR CLASS, REDUCED AND HANDED BACK (2026-09-23, MODE DECTET, SCRIP origin 7b74d4022, no SCRIP landing; telegram to hq_snobol4 on topic rtcc-r9-gva-safepoint-asymmetry-corrupts-locals).** ⛔ **THE CLAIM:** the rtcc veneer skips the r9 write-back under `RTCC_GLOBAL_R9_GVA` but reloads r9 unconditionally, so a function that repoints r9 to a local frame reads globals after any safe point. **THE MEASUREMENT:** (a) a census over `src/templates` and `src/emitter` finds 25 r9 writes and every one is a SIXTH-SysV-ARGUMENT load consumed within two lines by a veneered call whose reload restores `RT_GVA_VA`, or a push/pop pair around a trace hook; nothing repoints r9, and the emitter routes every local through frame slots (`graph_has_local` sets `op_gva_k` to -1 at emit.cpp 1114/1169/1301/1599/1606/1846); (b) at the IMAGE_driver crash gdb reads r9 = 0x70001000, the global array, intact; (c) BOTH reds are collector-invariant: identical at `SCRIP_HEAP_MB=512`, at 1, at `SCRIP_GC_STRESS=0`, and in mode 4. The asymmetry is BY DESIGN and sound while the flag holds. ⭐ **THE REAL CAUSES, each reduced against `/home/resources/x64/bin/sbl`:** REDEFINE_driver is an OPSYN semantics defect — OPSYN(new, old) must copy old's definition AT THE CALL and a later DEFINE of old must not follow; our two-literal compile-time path (lower_snobol4.c 2714) still resolves the redefined name (8-line reduction hits ERROR 246 where sbl prints `new[orig(x)]`), and the runtime path (pattern_match.c 500 → core.c 3818 `register_fn_alias`) with a null third argument returns null, the driver's exact `new[]`. IMAGE_driver is a function-frame codegen defect — an ALTERNATION inside a REPLACEMENT inside a DEFINEd function overwrites the return path with a DESCR word (rip = 0x100000002, IMAGE_γ/IMAGE_ω continuations beside it); 6-line reduction `IM BREAK('_') . T ('_' | 'Q') = T :S(IM1)F(RETURN)` segfaults in both modes at every arena, while the same replacement at top level, the same alternation match-only in the function, and `LEN(1)` in place of the alternation all pass. **LANE:** both are SNOBOL4-suite reds outside the collector class and hq_snobol4's under MODE line 2; handed back with the reductions verbatim; the cto reads a cure that lands in the shared emitter on request. **THE COUNT is untouched by this sitting.**
- **CTO-145 — THE FRAME MAP WAS ANCHORED AT THE POLL'S FLOOR, NOT THE FRAME'S BASE: EVERY CELL IN A FRAME READ EIGHT BYTES OUT OF PHASE WHENEVER THE SPINE WAS SHALLOWER THAN THE MAP'S DECLARED EXTENT, AND THAT WAS ROW 867's "SITE THAT REFUSES EVERY POLL FORM" AND hq_icon's TWO MODE-4 SEGFAULTS (2026-09-23, MODE DECTET, SCRIP 7b74d4022; rows 867 and chunk D ledgers; FINDING-2026-09-23-cto-the-frame-map-was-anchored-at-the-poll-floor-not-the-frame-base-so-every-cell-read-out-of-phase-when-the-spine-was-shallow.md; CEO-1181/1183).** ⭐ **THE COUNT: polled 195, partially_polled 0, unpolled 36, identity held, 231 sites — unchanged, no poll inserted this landing, baseline unchanged.** ⭐ **THE ONE GDB READ THE CEO ASKED FOR:** every concat result lives inside [g_hp_arena, g_hp_top) (arena 0x7fff6ca00000, results 0x7fff6ca0a850..8f0); the "second rw mapping" was the arena's live window split off by the quarantine trap's PROT_NONE above the top, the "2 GB reserve" strings were the slab's literal pool; the index is not the defect. ⭐ **THE HOLDER IS THE ZRES CELL AT floor+8 AND THE WALK MISSED IT:** `gc_walk_range` computed a frame's base as cell − map_off, CLAMPED it up to the poll's floor whenever the floor sat above it, and handed the clamped value to `gc_walk_interior` as the anchor — so every layout offset was applied from the return-address slot. The collector said so itself: `[GC-WALK-BADTAG] off=0 v=0xd0 p=0x900000002` (the poll site's low byte as the tag, the ZRES tag word as the pointer). gdb over 25 collections of the concat witness at stress 1: collections 4..12 (base eight bytes above the floor) walk the cell and update it when the block moves; 13 onward (anchor == floor) never visit it and the block reads 0xDB — exactly the three wrong output lines. ⭐ **THE CURE (gc_heap.c):** the anchor stays the true base; only the spine word walk starts at max(base, floor); a layout entry clipped at the floor is clipped on its own 16-byte cell grid (the floor is a return-address slot and always sits one word below the first live cell); the walk report carries `i_phase` (a bad-tag cell whose pointer word is itself a known tag). Gate `test_gate_gc_the_frame_map_anchor_is_the_frame_base_even_when_the_poll_floor_is_above_it.sh` (in `make test` after the concat gate, recorded in gate_wiring.tsv): red on 90392846a on all three arms (21 of 21 and 6 of 6 collections out of phase; mode-4 marklists rc=139), green here (phase 0 everywhere, floor-above-base exercised 10 and 3 times, marklists 21 collections). ⭐ **WHAT ELSE IT WAS:** hq_icon's Arizona mindfa (= jcon mindfa = IcnRungs procedure_record_every_replace_3) and ilib, mode 4 at 128 KB: control rc=139 both; cured tree byte-identical to .std (21 and 616 collections); the Icon arm at stress 1 gains procedure_record_every_replace_3 and _17 in both modes with zero cure-only names. ⛔ **THE :69 POLL STAYS OUT AND THE NEXT HOLDER IS NAMED:** with the walker cured the concat gate is GREEN with the poll restored (stress 0/1/3/5, collections 0/25/9/6), but the SNOBOL4 arm reads ONE cure-only red with it in — user_function_opsyn_8 prints an empty line for abcdef at stress 0 in both modes (3× on cure, 3× on a poll-only tree, control matches) — and the collector names the holder: five RAW spine words in the user-function call path (three copies of the HB_WSC name block "cat" at off −1424/−1384/−872, two of an HB_WSB workspace block at −1328/−728 of graph main), the bb_call_proc_staged family (chunk B). - **CTO-121 — THE ONE PLANT KNOB NOBODY USED DENIED A RUN OF BLOCKS, NOT THE nTH OF ITS KIND, AND I MEASURED THAT BEFORE WIRING IT RATHER THAN AFTER (2026-09-21 13:4x CDT `date`-read, cto; MODE TENET; effort max; SCRIP `67d69a015`, the third landing of the sitting; row minted on CEO-1064).** The ceo ruled the per-kind denial row mine and verified its premise themselves — zero scripts use `SCRIP_GC_PLANT_PIN_TYPE`. ⛔⭐⭐ **THE FIRST MEASUREMENT TAKEN AGAINST THE ROW'S GOAL REFUTED THE GOAL.** The forwarding pass advanced its counter only on blocks of the named kind but tested that counter against the skip value on EVERY marked block, so once the counter reached the skip at the first block of kind K, **every marked block after it was denied a forwarding address too**, until the next block of kind K moved the counter on. Measured at HEAD, stress 5, SKIP=1: `hb_arr.sno` TYPE=212 denied **3** blocks, TYPE=214 denied **2**, `hb_dtp.sno` TYPE=221 denied **4**, `hb_pldb.pl` TYPE=216 denied **4** — against exactly **1** with the knob unset. Held by **arm 5** of `test_gate_gc_the_plant_says_whether_it_applied`, proven RED against the pre-cure binary with those same widths and green after restoring it — A/B/A. ⭐ **WHY IT WAS CHEAP AND WOULD NOT HAVE STAYED CHEAP:** nobody had paid for it because no gate used the knob. A per-kind sensitivity arm wired to it would have denied a run of unrelated blocks and reported the witness's death as sensitivity to its OWN kind — an attribution the mechanism cannot support, **the same shape as arm 7 of the decidable test crediting a plant that never ran, found six hours earlier in the same sitting.** ⛔⭐ **AND THE WIRING IS DELIBERATELY NOT DONE.** With the cured knob the per-kind sweep reads **12 of 15 kinds DETECTED and three NOT — HB_AGGT and HB_AGGB on `hb_aggt.icn`, HB_DVEC on `hb_arr.sno`** — the plant applying in each case. Five more now exist (`PIN_FRONTIER_REG`, `PIN_CAS_TOP_REG`, `PIN_SIGMA_REG`, `PIN_CURSOR_REG`, `PIN_SUBJLEN_REG`), each naming its register, its role and the sites that read it; **proven to fail once** — renaming the subject pin to r12 fails the build at `x86_asm.h:360` with the assert's own message. The gate `test_gate_gc_rbx_is_the_frontier.sh` (wired blocking, adopted into the wiring floor) keeps step 1's claim: every rbx spelling in the templates and the emitter is in a declared set of three, a fourth reds, no template spells the push-rbx saver, and `FAIL_ONCE=1` plants a scratch use and is seen. The emitted output is **byte-identical** (20 Icon benchmark artifacts unchanged, 0 updated), which is the honest claim for a landing that declares rather than changes. ⛔ **THE FLIP CANNOT BE DELIVERED AS THE ROW WRITES IT, AND THE REASON IS A MEASUREMENT: EMITTED CODE NEVER CALLS THE ALLOCATOR.** Zero mentions of `rt_gcheap_alloc`, `rt_ws_alloc`, `rt_heap_alloc`, `rt_pm_struct_alloc` or `rt_agg_alloc` in `src/templates` or `src/emitter`; the safe-point census reads 244 allocating call sites in emitted code and every one is a call into a runtime entry that allocates internally (1655 of 8645 runtime functions reach the allocator); the GOT load the row wants gone lives in `rtx_alloc.s` 7/46/60 and `rtx_str.s` 100, inside the runtime. So no emitted instruction reads or writes the frontier, a cache of it has no consumer, and the arm that reads the emitted `.s` for an advancing rbx presumes a path that does not exist. A cache only pays where the consumer shares the register world: the runtime may hold the frontier in rbx across its own internals because SysV preserves it, but every call from emitted code arrives with the emitted world's rbx and must save and restore it, which costs exactly the memory round trip the cache was to remove. Two designs are with the ceo — an emitted inline fast path for compile-time-constant allocation shapes with the write-back/reload discipline (the real prize, and the allocator's fast path then written twice and held equal by a gate), or defer the flip to its own row with a written design, which is my recommendation because it is a speed win and not a correctness fix while the acceptance path still holds hq_prolog's findall red.
- **CTO-92 LANDED — rbx STEP 1: EVERY SCRATCH USE IS OFF THE REGISTER, AND THE FIFTH SITE WAS DEAD CODE (2026-09-20 08:xx CDT, cto; MODE SEPTET; row `gc-rbx-is-the-bump-frontier-...`, ceo CEO-959/974; Lon: *"RBX was always meant to be the GC bump frontier. If it is not so, make it so."*; SCRIP `25d0955d8`, corpus `9a69dfcc2`).** ⭐ **A RESTORATION, NOT A NEW DESIGN:** the frontier lived in the HEAP arm of the `ZC_PORT_*` selector; when the no-modes law deleted those arms the register plane went with them, and rbx — preserved by SysV and therefore free-looking — was picked up as scratch by four live sites. **THE ALIGNED-CALL SAVER NEEDS NO CALLEE-SAVED REGISTER AT ALL:** one shared pair of helpers (`x86_align_call_enter`/`x86_align_call_leave`) parks the caller's rsp ON the aligned stack, so rsp is 16-aligned at the call as the ABI requires and the restore works on every path that jumps to the epilogue; r11 holds the parked pointer for three instructions before a call that clobbers it anyway. The two sites that READ through the saver re-base on r11 with every offset shifted 8 down, and the four scan boxes take r11 for a shuffle the preceding call has already clobbered. ⛔ **THE FIFTH SITE THE CENSUS NAMED DOES NOT EXIST IN THE LIVE TREE:** `emit_aligned_call_rt` had a declaration, a definition and ZERO callers — DELETED under the dead-code rule, which is also why its hand-encoded binary arm never needed re-encoding. **MEASURED:** the emitted asm of an Icon scan witness carries zero rbx; the three trace-exercising Icon rungs entries match their refs on stdout in both media and their `&trace` streams agree with each other and byte-for-byte with icont's own (the arm that actually grades the re-basing); the seven smokes 0 FAIL; the GC witness battery 104 of 108 in BOTH media at the tiny arena (the four = hq_prolog's findall row); `make test-arena` 43 arms 2 red, both pre-existing and attributed; the sno-chain gate 8/8; preflight 56/0; 20 of 23 Icon benchmark artifacts regenerated, 3522 insertions against 3522 deletions, one shape twice per site; **IcnRungs at 1 MB m3 824/2/0 m4 825/1/0 and SnoRungs m3 1963/2/0 m4 1963/2/0 — not one entry moved from the two landings before this one.** NAMED AND NOT MINE: `test_gate_picker_lane_table_agrees_with_mode.sh` went red at the SEPTET flip and was red on origin under the same reading; the ceo cured it at `5367103df` while this landing was in verdict.

The two trampolines now seed r13 from `Σ` and r15 from `Σlen` — the live outer subject mid-match, NULL/0 when cold — the same both-directions argument they already made for r12 against the dcap base. | port | sites | OWNED live | PASS-THRU | owned/site |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| α | 1375 | 1612 | 6576 | 1.17 |
| β | 581 | 68 | 3364 | **0.12** |
| γ | 175 | 178 | 871 | 1.02 |
| ω | 175 | 177 | 713 | 1.01 |

  - ⛔ **ω IS NOT EMPTY — BUT THE HEADLINE NUMBER IS THE ABI, NOT A POINTER.** **160 of the 177 owned readings at ω are `rbp`**, a frame base: a stack address, provably not a heap pointer (§ 6.5a's RSP/LEA class). Strip it and the owned residual over all 175 ω ports is **seventeen**. And the number that actually decides whether a port poll owes a record is `r13`, the one register § 6.5a measured holding a collected-heap pointer: **`r13` is live at 15 of 175 ω ports (2 owned, 13 passed through).** ⭐ **So the ceo's (b) holds for the large majority and fails for a small, NAMED minority — the ω edges where the enclosing match's subject is still live.**
  - ⭐ **THE GAP IN THE REASONING, which is why it had to be measured rather than argued: the claim is about the BOX's contribution, but the poll's obligation is about the REGISTER FILE.** At a box's concede its own ζ cells are dead — and `r13` still holds the ENCLOSING MATCH's subject base, an ancestor's value and not the conceding box's to abandon. *"The box is finished"* and *"no register needs carrying"* are two different statements, and only the second licenses dropping the record.
  - ⛔ **THE AGGREGATE IS ONE LANGUAGE: 160 of the 175 ω ports are PROLOG's**; the other seven frontends contribute 15 between them (Icon 3, Raku 3, SNOBOL4 2, Pascal 2, Rebus 2, Snocone 2, deferred-SNOBOL4 1). Any conclusion read off the total is a conclusion about Prolog's lowering — the same failure as a census whose population lacks the class it grades, in the other direction.
  - ⛔ **AND THE CAVEAT THAT GOVERNS EVERY NUMBER ABOVE:** a `ret` makes all six callee-saved registers live by the SysV contract, so a port sitting before a `ret` reads every register live even where the value's last read is behind it. **An OWNED count at a port is an UPPER BOUND**, and `rbp` dominating one is the ABI talking. ## THE CTO LOOP
1. Session start as every seat: `fetch` + `ff-only` merge of SCRIP, corpus, .github; `bash SCRIP/scripts/s4e_msg.sh check`; print the banner (`python3 .github/scripts/util_suite_banner.py`).
2. Resume the claimed row (`s4e_msg.sh next` resumes your own claim). One problem at a time; the ceo names the next from the suite table when this one lands (the class blocking the most package programs across lanes).
3. ASM-DIFF-FIRST (RULES.md § Debugging order), the three zetas (ARCH-ENGINE.md), zero comments, LF, `-O0`, no new globals without Lon's banner grant, `make` incremental for the verdict.
4. Push per rung with the ledger line before the next step — the account change is abrupt (ARCH-FLEET-CEO.md § TWO OPERATING MODES, CEO-324); nothing lives only in this session.
5. Land: rebase, re-prove, push code repos then `.github`; FINDING named for the claim; move this LIVE CURSOR; `s4e_msg.sh done <topic>` (computed); telegram `ceo` with the hash.
6. The CTO does not audit, arbitrate, rank, or write law. Escalations go to `ceo/inbox`; Lon's in-chat word wins immediately and is routed into this file the same session.
